## Review

**7 out of 10, Effective.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck follows a clear route (agenda, introduction, strategy update, conclusion) and keeps one consistent identity: blue palette, section-tag badges on slides 14 to 25, and speaker divider slides. Slides 8, 21 and 25 are well built: labelled charts, a before and after proof of concept, and a lever strip tied to a CHF 50m and CHF 90m figure. Weaknesses are real, though. Many pages (6, 11, 15, 17, 20, 22, 31) use very small body text with dense boxes, the strategy-priority slide 11 is hard to take in at a glance, and slide 8 has an unexplained '11%' bar and no stated takeaway in its title. The deck reads as an investor-day pack, so the density is partly justified, but headings are mostly topic labels and the pages lean on a presenter to explain them.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the consistent section-badge system, the four-step lever strip on slide 25, and the R&D to Retail to Patient flow with before and after proof on slide 21.

**Most useful repair:** Raise the smallest text and cut words on slides 6, 11, 15, 20 and 31, and label or explain the 11% marker on slide 8.

**What would change the score:** Rewrite topic titles such as 'Recent highlights' and 'Growing need for hearing care solutions across regions' as takeaway statements, and replace the three near-empty name-only divider slides (13, 18, 23, 26) with content or merge them.

_Confidence medium. 36 of 36 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

The deck opens with a title slide and a forward-looking statements disclaimer, then an agenda in four parts: introduction, strategy update, conclusion and Q&A. The introduction reviews the first six months under the new CEO, covering market and technology leadership, financial strength and recent product highlights, and sets out a demographic case for steady market growth. The strategy update covers the planned divestment of Consumer Hearing, a CHF 6 billion revenue ambition with five strategic priorities, the leadership team, R&D and innovation, an Asia playbook, channel strategy with wholesale and retail synergies, operations and productivity, finance and trading update, mid-term targets, and capital allocation. The conclusion restates the CHF 6 billion ambition for FY 2030/31 and the focus on hearing care. Slides worth studying include the market-growth slide with its population and adoption charts, the wholesale-retail synergy slide with its before-and-after proof of concept, and the productivity slide that ties four levers to a cost figure.

## Questions about this deck

**How many slides are in the Sonova presentation deck?**

The Sonova deck has 36 slides. Slide 3 presents the agenda in four parts: Introduction, Strategy update, Conclusion and Q&A.

**What revenue ambition does the Sonova deck set out and by when?**

Sonova sets a CHF 6 billion revenue ambition, as shown on slide 11. Slide 35 states the goal is reaching CHF 6 billion in revenues by FY 2030/31.

**What does the Sonova deck say about the wholesale and retail synergy pilot results?**

Slide 21 includes a proof of concept with more than 100 appointments in a first 4-week pilot. The same slide also shows a +20% Return on Ad Spend figure in its before-and-after comparison.

**What are the four parts of the Sonova presentation agenda?**

Slide 3 lists the agenda as 1. Introduction, 2. Strategy update, 3. Conclusion and 4. Q&A. The Strategy update section is presented by Eric Bernard, Anders Rosengren, Roberto di Fiore and Elodie Carr-Cingari.

**Layout**

Four navy rounded cards in a two-by-two grid occupy the left half, each with a bold-and-regular heading at top-left, an icon at bottom-right and a caption below. A vertical blue divider with a blue plus circle sits at about 65% of the width, separating the grid from a right-hand column headed Clear upsides from: with a bulleted list. The title is top-left at about 8% from the top with a subtitle beneath; the sonova logo sits bottom-left and the page number bottom-right, with margins of about 3% on the left and 2% on the right.

**Visuals**

Four rounded-rectangle filled shapes in navy #003C64 each carrying a white line icon: top-left card shows a puzzle piece held in two hands outline icon (the only raster image, 50 by 50 pixels); top-right card shows a flag above a group of three people outline icon; bottom-left card shows a computer chip outline icon with pins and a central square; bottom-right card shows a safe or vault door outline icon with a round dial. No charts, tables or photographs.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background with no gradient or texture. Titles and accent text are blue #007FBE; body and subheads are dark navy #003C64; card fills are navy #003C64 with white text. Typeface is Roboto (sans-serif) in Regular for the title, subtitle, captions and bullets, and Bold for the first line of each card heading. Title size about 4.8% of slide height, body about 2.6%, card headings about 3.0%. Cards have small corner radii of about 1% of slide height, flat with no shadows. FY 24/25) / Infinio Ultra / DESIGN / AWARD / 2026 / sonova / HEAR THE WORLD / 6

**Layout**

Single row of three equal white cards spanning the lower two-thirds of the slide, with the title top-left above them. Cards are left-aligned at about 3% and 67.8% of slide width with equal gutters of about 1.2%, and their tops sit at about 26.8% and bottoms at about 89.5% of slide height. Title left edge at about 2.9% width, aligned with the left card edge area; sonova logo bottom-left at about 3% x and 95% y; slide number bottom-right at about 95.4% x and 95.9% y. No footer text other than the logo and page number.

**Visuals**

Four photographs and one award logo, all placed inside the cards. (1) Virto R: a single black hearing aid (in-the-ear shell, about 11% by 20% of slide) shown from an angled top view, glossy black with a circular grey centre cap with a white three-spoke emblem, on white background, lit from above with soft highlights, not cropped, sitting to the right of the figures in the Virto R card. (2) iF DESIGN AWARD 2026 logo: square-cornered red (#E8363B) rectangle split into an upper block with a white stylised 'iF' mark and a lower block reading DESIGN / AWARD / 2026 in white bold sans-serif capitals, about 6% by 21% of slide, centred in the Awards card. (3) Infinio Ultra product: a tan/brown behind-the-ear hearing aid with a green-outlined oval badge reading Infinio Ultra overlaid at its upper left, shown standing on a pale reflective surface, white background, about 11% by 20% of slide, lower left of Awards card. (4) Charging case: an open oval bronze/champagne case with a black lid and a small in-ear device inside, reflective white surface below, about 11% by 20% of slide, lower right of Awards card. No charts, tables or diagrams.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background (#FFFFFF). Titles and card headings in medium blue #0E82C6 to #007FBE, regular weight, Roboto sans-serif; title about 4.8% of slide height, card headings about 3.7%. Body text in dark navy #003C64, Roboto Regular with bold lead-ins, about 2.6% of slide height; bullets in blue #007FBE. Cards are white rounded rectangles with small corner radius (about 0.3% of slide height) and soft light-grey drop shadow, thin light-grey edge. Big numbers in navy #003C64 Roboto Regular about 5.2% of slide height. No gradients. Logo in blue #007FBE. Page number in grey #757266.

**Layout**

Four equal columns of centred content sit below a full-width blue wave header; the title is left-aligned across the top band at about 7.5% from the top with a left margin of about 2.9%. Each column holds a white circular icon badge, a centred two-line heading, a thin blue rule and a centred caption. The sonova logo sits bottom-left and the slide number bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Four white circular badges with thin soft shadows, each containing a single-colour icon: (1) a dark navy globe with a blue continent pattern beside two overlapping blue and navy human head silhouettes, bottom-left of the globe; (2) a blue faceted diamond resting in an open navy outlined hand; (3) a navy and blue balance scale with a blue central post and base, with two blue filled pans; (4) a blue and navy outline of a human ear with a blue inner ear shape. Background is a full-width blue wave graphic with fine line patterns in the top portion (about top 46% of the slide). Sonova logo: blue wordmark 'sonova' in bold italic with 'HEAR THE WORLD' beneath in small blue capitals. Thin blue horizontal rules (#0283CA) under each heading, about 0.2% high, spanning each column width.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF lower area with a blue wave graphic (deep blue #0A5FA0 to bright blue #1A8FD6) across the top of the slide.

### Slide 8

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Pairs a population projection with a severity-by-region adoption table so the growth argument is visible in one view.
- Review: Slides 8, 21 and 25 are well built: labelled charts, a before and after proof of concept, and a lever strip tied to a CHF 50m and CHF 90m figure.
- Review: Many pages (6, 11, 15, 17, 20, 22, 31) use very small body text with dense boxes, the strategy-priority slide 11 is hard to take in at a glance, and slide 8 has an unexplained '11%' bar and no stated takeaway in its title.
- Suggested repair: Raise the smallest text and cut words on slides 6, 11, 15, 20 and 31, and label or explain the 11% marker on slide 8.

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white slide: a stacked-bar chart card on the left (x 2.9 to 42.2 percent) and a table card on the right (x 51.6 to 91.9 percent), both starting at about y 29.5 percent. The title sits top-left at x 2.9, y 7.7, with left-aligned section headings above each card at y about 24 percent; the sources line sits bottom-left at y 87 percent, the sonova logo sits bottom-left below it, and the page number '8' sits bottom-right at x 95.4, y 95.9.

**Visuals**

Left: stacked column chart titled 'Million individuals', with no printed y-axis or axis range and a thin grey baseline. Three columns: 2015 total 617 (Developed 224 bottom in blue, Emerging 393 top in dark teal); 2025 total 857 (Developed 287, Emerging 570); 2035 total 1’169 (Developed 332, Emerging 837). Totals are printed above each column; segment values are printed inside. Legend below: blue square = Developed (NA, Europe, ANZ, Japan); dark teal square = Emerging (China, India, Southeast Asia, Latam, MEA). Right of the chart is a growth panel with column headers 2015-25 and 2025-35 (underlined); Absolute growth: +240 and +312 in navy rounded badges; CAGR: +3.3% and +3.1% in navy rounded badges. No highlighted points. Right: table with columns (unlabelled row header), North America & Western Europe, and Other markets¹. Rows: Severe 67% / 10%; Moderate 44% / 5%; Mild 19% / 2%; Total 38% / 4% (Total values in blue). Thin light-grey row dividers separate rows. A navy horizontal rule with a centred navy badge reading 11% sits across the bottom of the table card, overlapping its lower edge.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean corporate look on white. Title in Roboto Regular, blue #007FBE, about 4.8 percent of slide height. Section headings in Roboto Regular, navy #003C64, about 3.0 percent. Body and table text Roboto Regular in navy #003C64 at about 2.6 percent; table values Roboto Bold. Data labels inside bars are white; totals and years use #002060 or #003C64. Accent blue #007FBE for Developed bars and Total values; emerging segments #295C7D. Section headings have a thin navy underline. Cards are white rounded rectangles with a soft grey drop shadow and a corner radius of about 3 percent of slide width. Growth and severity values are set in navy rounded badges with white bold text. Sources line Roboto with bold 'Sources:' label at about 1.7 percent.

### Slide 9

**Text**

2. Strategy update

**Layout**

A single title line sits in the vertical middle of the slide, starting at about 27.8% from the left and ending at about 66.6%, so it is close to but slightly left of centre. The title is left-aligned inside its box. There is no footer, no slide number and no other text, and the wave artwork runs full-bleed to every edge.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed decorative illustration covering the whole slide: dense, thin, light-blue sinuous lines forming overlapping wave bands. The bands sweep from the upper left down toward the lower right and rise again toward the upper right. The lines are brightest in the upper right and fade into the darker blue of the lower left. The illustration is not a photograph, has no subject, is not cropped, and has no boxed frame. It has no logos, icons, charts or tables.

**Design**

Background: Solid #003C64 deep blue with a blue gradient and a full-bleed wave-line illustration over it. Solid deep blue base #003C64 with a subtle diagonal gradient, darker at the lower left (about #004A78) and lighter at the upper right (about #0E7CC0). Overlaid wave lines in light blue (about #1E8AD6) at low opacity with hairline strokes of roughly 1 px at 1920 px width. Title text is white (#FFFFFF) in Roboto Regular, regular weight, sentence case, no italics, no capitals, no letter spacing, at about 8.16% of slide height. No accent colours, shapes, shadows or corner radii.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

- shape at x 0, y 0, w 100, h 100 [#003C64]: Full-slide solid rectangle filling the background (Covers the whole slide, no border, no shadow, no opacity change.)
- illustration at x 0, y 0, w 100, h 100 [#1E8AD6]: Decorative wave-line illustration of many thin light-blue sinuous lines forming overlapping wave bands across the whole slide (Full-bleed image of 1920 by 1080 pixels, not cropped, no boxed frame, lines at low opacity over the blue base, brightest at upper right and fading toward lower left, no overlay tint beyond the base colour.)

### Slide 10

**Text**

Sharpening focus by divesting Consumer Hearing / Background / Rationale for the decision / In 2022, Sonova acquired Sennheiser’s Consumer Division to explore hearables and early entry consumer hearing solutions / Consumer Hearing Business strengths are in audiophile, premium headphones and sound bar segments / Consumer audio has its own market logic, with distinct channels, product cycles, and economics versus hearing aids / Next: / Structured process to find a fit-for-purpose ownership / sonova HEAR THE WORLD / 10

**Layout**

Single-row three-column layout of equal-height cards beneath a left-aligned title, with generous white space above the cards and a logo bottom-left and page number bottom-right. The title sits top-left at about 7.5% from the top with a left margin near 3%. The three cards are roughly aligned on a common baseline at about 75% of slide height, with the header bars straddling each card's top edge. A blue curved arrow and chevron sit between the second and third cards, overlapping the gap above the third card's top edge.

**Visuals**

Three header bars each contain a white rounded-square icon tile on the right. Left bar icon: a clockwise-and-counterclockwise circular arrow around a clock face (history/time), drawn as a navy outline. Middle bar icon: two arrows crossing, one pointing up-right and one pointing down, forming a branching swap symbol, navy outline. Right bar icon: outline headphones with two ear cups in blue-navy outline. Between the middle and right cards there is a solid blue (#007FBE) curved arrow sweeping right, followed by a dark navy (#003C64) double chevron pointing right. No photographs, charts or tables.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background (#FFFFFF). Title in Roboto Regular, blue #007FBE. Body text in Roboto Regular, navy #003C64, with key phrases in Roboto Bold; the third card's text and bold phrase are in blue #007FBE. Header bars are navy #003C64 (left and middle) and blue #007FBE (right) with white Roboto Regular labels. Title is about 4.8% of slide height; body text about 2.6%; header labels about 3.0%. Cards are white rectangles with thin light grey border and a soft drop shadow, small corner radius. Header bars have rounded corners of about 1% of slide width. Icon tiles are white rounded squares with small corner radius. Page number in grey #757266.

### Slide 11

**Review notes**

- Review: Many pages (6, 11, 15, 17, 20, 22, 31) use very small body text with dense boxes, the strategy-priority slide 11 is hard to take in at a glance, and slide 8 has an unexplained '11%' bar and no stated takeaway in its title.
- Suggested repair: Raise the smallest text and cut words on slides 6, 11, 15, 20 and 31, and label or explain the 11% marker on slide 8.

**Text**

A CHF 6 billion revenue ambition
Powered by

priorities
Innovate for
adoption

• Leverage synergies between hearing aids and cochlear implants

11

**Layout**

Three-column card layout between a title block at top and an enablers band at bottom. The title sits top-left at about x 3%, y 8%, with the subtitle 'Powered by' below it at y 15%, both left-aligned on a 3% left margin. A navy vertical label panel sits at the left (x 5% to 13.5%, y 28% to 67%) beside three equal white cards (about x 16% to 39.5%, 43.5% to 67%, 71% to 94.5%, y 28% to 67%). A horizontal grey rule runs at y 71% across the content width (x 5% to 95%) with a navy chevron disc centred on it at x 49%. Below, a blue Enablers label (x 5% to 13.5%, y 75% to 88%) sits beside a full-width blue bar (x 16% to 95%, y 75% to 80%) and a segmented five-cell bar (y 82% to 88%). The sonova logo sits bottom-left at x 3%, y 94%; the page number sits bottom-right at x 95%, y 96%.

**Visuals**

Three line icons in white circular badges, each overlapping the top-left corner of its card header. Icon 1 (x 16.6%, y 30.4%, 7.1 by 12.5 percent): a navy outline lightbulb with rays, centred in a white circle with soft grey shadow. Icon 2 (x 44.1%, y 30.4%): a navy outline triangle with two horizontal stripes (a pyramid/layers symbol) in a white circle with soft shadow. Icon 3 (x 71.7%, y 30.4%): a navy outline gear containing a small line-chart with an upward trend, in a white circle with soft shadow. Navy chevron in a navy disc on the horizontal rule at x 49%, y 69%. No photographs, charts or tables.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with flat, clean corporate styling. Title colour light blue #007FBE in Roboto Regular, about 4.8% of slide height (cap height about 3.4%). Subtitle 'Powered by' in navy #003C64 Roboto Regular, about 3.3%. Card headings in navy #003C64 Roboto Bold, about 2.6%. Bullet text navy #003C64 Roboto Regular with bold keywords, about 2.2%. Bullet glyphs are small dark-green #0E3E1B ArialMT dots. Left label panel and card-stripe navy #003C64; enabler elements blue #0283CA with white Roboto Regular text about 2.6%. Cards are white rectangles with 0.1% light grey #D9D9D9 border and soft drop shadow, corner radius about 0.3%. **Layout**

Single-slide organisation chart on a white field with a left-aligned title and subtitle at the top, both starting at about 2.9% from the left edge. A top-down tree of photo cards runs from the CEO card at top centre, through two rows of executive cards, into two tinted panels at the bottom: a wide left panel for the Hearing Instruments regions and a narrower right panel for Business Units. The sonova logo sits bottom left, and the page number sits bottom right at about 95% from the left.

**Visuals**

Organisation chart made of thin light-grey elbow connectors and about 17 rounded-corner portrait photographs with small national flags. Top tier: one CEO portrait (E. Bernard) centred at top, a French flag at its upper-left corner. Second tier: four executive cards in a row, CFO E. Carr-Cingari (Swiss and French flags stacked at left), CHRO K. Kruglova (UK flag), R&D A. Rosengren (Swedish flag), COO R. di Fiore (Italian flag). Third tier: Group Strategy B. Christ (Danish flag), CMO R. Shroff (Indian flag), VP Retail Excellence A. Schmidlechner (Austrian flag), and a blue TBH placeholder box labelled CDO. Bottom panels: under the Hearing Instruments bar, four regional cards with portraits and flags: EMEA & LATAM O. Lux (Austrian flag), North America N. Dessypris (US flag), APAC ex. China B. Wagner (German flag), China F. Fang (Chinese flag). Under the Business Units bar: Cochlear Implants A. Simpson (UK flag) and Consumer Hearing L. Beck (Australian flag). Photographs are head-and-shoulders portraits in business attire, mostly on blurred grey, office or glass-window backgrounds, with soft natural light; the CEO and R&D portraits have dark backgrounds. Portraits are cropped into rounded rectangles, not full-bleed. A pair of short blue slashes (//) marks a break in the connector line above the Business Units bar. Dominant colours: dark navy, pale blue, and grey.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in blue #007FBE, subtitle and body labels in dark navy #003C64, small heading 'Selected Executive Committee members' in blue #0283CA. Typeface is Roboto (sans-serif): Regular for titles and names, Bold for role labels and region headings. Title is about 4.8% of slide height, subtitle about 3.3%, labels about 2.2%. Connectors are light grey (about #D9D9D9) lines roughly 0.3% of slide height thick. Photo cards have rounded corners of about 1% of slide height radius. TBH box is solid blue #007FBE with white text. Hearing Instruments header bar is dark navy #003C64 with white text. Business Units header bar is dark teal #005A64 with white text. Left panel is pale blue #D6EFFF at 47% opacity, right panel is teal #007F8D at 10% opacity, both with rounded corners. No shadows or gradients.

### Slide 13

**Text**

Anders Rosengren
Group Vice President, R&D
13

**Layout**

Single centered title block on a blank white slide: name centered horizontally around the middle-upper third, subtitle centered beneath it. Sonova logo bottom-left corner and slide number bottom-right, with the background wave pattern filling the whole slide.

**Visuals**

Decorative background illustration of flowing wave lines: many thin pale-blue (light cyan-blue) curved lines forming sweeping S-shaped wave bands that cross the slide from left to right, densest in the upper-left and lower-right, fading to near-white toward the centre. The waves are full-bleed, behind all text, with no boxes or borders. Small Sonova logo at bottom-left: the lowercase wordmark 'sonova' in blue with the tagline 'HEAR THE WORLD' beneath in small caps. No photographs or charts.

**Design**

Background white (#FFFFFF) with pale blue wave lines (about #CFE6F5 to #E8F3FA). Title and subtitle both in blue #007FBE. Slide number in grey #757266. Typeface Roboto Regular (sans-serif), normal weight, no italics, no capitals. Title text about 8.2% of slide height (font size 8.16% of height); subtitle about 4.4% of height. Wave lines are thin hairlines with no shadows or gradients.

### Slide 14

**Review notes**

- Review: The deck follows a clear route (agenda, introduction, strategy update, conclusion) and keeps one consistent identity: blue palette, section-tag badges on slides 14 to 25, and speaker divider slides.

**Text**

Shaping the industry through customer-led
and research-driven innovation
Innovate for
adoption
Leading
audiological research
26

in FY25/26

EasyGuard™

and up to 38% reduced service requirements.

innovation
More compact form
factors with real-time AI
More aesthetic,
lifestyle-aligned solutions
AI functionalities
expansion beyond
speech-in-noise
sonova
HEAR THE WORLD
14

**Layout**

Three-column layout on a white slide: a left column headed 'Leading audiological research' with three stacked statistics, a centre column of three stacked white product cards, and a right column headed 'Next wave innovation' with three bullet statements. Two thin vertical blue rules separate the columns, with a blue chevron circle sitting on each rule at mid-height. The title is left-aligned at top left, the 'Innovate for adoption' badge sits top right, the sonova logo is bottom left and the page number is bottom right, with margins of about 3 percent on the left and right.

**Visuals**

Three product photographs, one per centre card, and one lightbulb icon in the top-right badge. Card 1 (Functionality, Sphere): a bronze-brown receiver-in-canal style hearing aid with a thin clear tube and a small grey button, shown at an angle on a light grey background; the right edge is cropped by the card edge, not full-bleed. Card 2 (Design, Virto R): a glossy black behind-the-ear hearing aid with a circular emblem with a white line motif and a small slot on the top right, shown from an angle, bottom-right corner cropped by the card edge; soft studio lighting with highlights on the curved surface. Card 3 (Usability, EasyGuard): a translucent grey-clear dome with a flower-like petal ring and a dark interior, shown close-up, cropped at the bottom right by the card edge; cool grey dominant tones with white highlights. Lightbulb icon: navy outline bulb with three short rays above it, inside a white circle with a soft grey shadow. Blue chevron icons: two white right-pointing chevrons inside solid blue circles, one on each vertical rule. The faint image frames behind the cards are soft-edged shadow images with no visible content of their own.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background #FFFFFF. Titles and left and right column headings are in blue #007FBE; card headings and statistics are in bold dark navy #003C64; body text is regular navy #003C64; accents (short dash bars, vertical rules, chevron circles, sonova logo) are blue #007FBE. Typeface is Roboto throughout: Regular for titles and body, Bold for card headings and statistics. Main title is about 4.8 percent of slide height; column headings and card headings about 3.7 percent; body text about 2.2 percent; page number about 1.7 percent. Cards are white rounded rectangles with a soft grey drop shadow and corner radius of about 0.8 percent of slide width. Dash bars are short solid blue strokes about 2.3 percent of slide width and 0.5 percent of slide height. Vertical rules are hairline blue lines.

### Slide 15

**Review notes**

- Review: The deck follows a clear route (agenda, introduction, strategy update, conclusion) and keeps one consistent identity: blue palette, section-tag badges on slides 14 to 25, and speaker divider slides.
- Suggested repair: Raise the smallest text and cut words on slides 6, 11, 15, 20 and 31, and label or explain the 11% marker on slide 8.

**Text**

Hearing care fosters healthy living / – backed by years of scientific engagement / Innovate for / adoption / Selected results from recent pioneering studies using Infinio and Sphere / Quality of life / Social well-being / Physical well-being / Cognitive well-being / Ease of listening / Speech intelligibility / Audibility / Positive impact on social participation with Sphere: / Less fatigue and higher social engagement. / (pioneering research, published in 2025) / Improved ability to memorize words / in dynamic conversations. / Significantly reduced listening effort measured via / blood flow in the brain and by subjective rating / (pioneering research, published in 2026) / Outstanding speech intelligibility even / in dynamic fast paced conversations / (pioneering research published 2024) / Exceptional sound quality and / spontaneous acceptance. / Additional information available at: https://www.phonak.com/en-int/professionals/audiology-hub/evidence-library / sonova / HEAR THE WORLD / 15

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white field: a seven-step staircase of stacked white boxes occupies the left half, each step one row higher and set further right, with its label right-aligned inside the box; five icon-and-text rows stack in the right half. The title sits top-left in two lines with a subtitle beneath it, and a rounded 'Innovate for adoption' badge sits top-right. The sonova logo is bottom-left, the link line sits bottom-right of the rows, and the slide number is bottom-right at the margin.

**Visuals**

Seven stepped white rectangles forming an ascending staircase, each with a light grey-blue drop shadow, labelled Audibility (bottom), Speech intelligibility, Ease of listening, Cognitive well-being, Physical well-being, Social well-being and Quality of life (top). Five small square icons with rounded light-grey borders, each about 6% of slide width: (1) a navy line-drawn network of five person icons linked by lines on white; (2) a light-grey-framed box plot chart with three box-and-whisker groups in light blue and mid blue, no readable axis values; (3) a dark blue photograph-like illustration of a brain in cyan-blue wireframe on a deep navy background, with a small caption-like label at its bottom edge; (4) a box plot chart titled CCOSA with about four box-and-whisker groups in light blue on white, a small legend at lower right, axis label Test Sequence at bottom; (5) a bar chart titled Sound Quality with two groups of bars, Speaker's Voice and Ceiling Voice, light blue and mid blue bars with labels about 20 to 80 on the y-axis range 0 to 100. A lightbulb line icon in navy inside a white circle with a soft shadow, sitting at the left of the Innovate badge. No photographs otherwise. The sonova wordmark logo in bold italic blue with the tagline HEAR THE WORLD below it.

**Design**

Background: Plain white #FFFFFF. Clean corporate look on white. Title in Roboto Regular, blue #007FBE, large (about 4.8% of slide height, cap height about 3.4%). Subtitle and all body text in Roboto Regular, navy #003C64, about 3.0% of slide height. Bold lead-in words in Roboto Bold, navy #003C64. Source and citation lines in Roboto Regular at about 2.0% in navy #003C64; slide number in Roboto Regular grey #757266 about 1.7%. Staircase steps are white with thin light grey edges and soft blue-grey drop shadows, square corners. Innovate badge is a white rounded rectangle with a navy #003C64 outline about 2 px thick and corner radius about 0.5% of width, text Roboto Bold navy. Icons have rounded square frames with light grey #D9D9D9 borders about 1 px thick, corner radius about 1.5% of width. Link is underlined navy.

**Layout**

Title sits top-left in two lines at about 3% left margin, with the 'Innovate for adoption' badge top-right. Below, a 3-by-2 grid of six rounded cards on the left (columns at x 1.8, 21.6, 41.2 percent; rows at y 32.6 and 66.7 percent) with a grey chevron circle at the right edge of the top row; a bold-led bullet list occupies the right column from x 64 to 95 percent. **Visuals**

Six white rounded rectangular cards (381 by 196 pixel placed images, each with a soft grey drop shadow) arranged as a 3-by-2 grid: top row DSP, Proprietary DNN chip, Data pipeline and training; bottom row Algorithm, Full system integration, Audiological competence. Connectors: thin blue horizontal lines link DSP to Proprietary DNN chip and Proprietary DNN chip to Data pipeline and training; a thin blue vertical line links DSP down to Algorithm and Data pipeline and training down to Audiological competence; thin blue horizontal lines link Algorithm to Full system integration and Full system integration to Audiological competence. A green photographic render of a DEEPSONIC chip (a square processor package with a glowing green circuit-board die, dark grey bevelled edge, slight shadow beneath, on a white background with a faint hexagonal glow) sits in the centre of the grid overlapping the bottom edge of the Proprietary DNN chip card and the top edge of the Full system integration card. A light grey circle with a white right-pointing chevron sits at the right of the top row, overlapping the edge of the Data pipeline card. A white circular badge with a navy outline lightbulb icon sits to the left of the Innovate for adoption outlined box. The sonova logo is a blue italic wordmark with the tagline HEAR THE WORLD beneath it.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background #FFFFFF. Titles and subheading in blue #007FBE; card labels and body bullets in dark navy #003C64; bullet dots blue #007FBE; bold lead-ins in dark navy #003C64. Typeface Roboto, a sans-serif family: Regular for titles and card labels, Bold for bullet lead-ins and badge text. Title text is about 4.8 percent of slide height, card labels and body text about 2.6 to 3.0 percent, page number about 1.7 percent. Connector lines are thin blue #007FBE lines of about 1 pt. Cards are white rounded rectangles with small corner radius and soft grey shadow. The badge is a navy #003C64 outlined rounded rectangle with about 2 pt stroke and small corner radius. Gradients: none.

### Slide 17

**Review notes**

- Review: The deck follows a clear route (agenda, introduction, strategy update, conclusion) and keeps one consistent identity: blue palette, section-tag badges on slides 14 to 25, and speaker divider slides.

**Text**

Driving adoption by bridging audiology and lifestyle
Expand design beyond RIC – Toward lifestyle, healthy-aging & connected platforms.

17

**Layout**

Four cards in a staggered two-tier arrangement on a loose grid: the upper pair (left, x 2.9% and 26.8%) sits above the lower pair (x 50.7% and 74.6%), with two thin blue horizontal rules at about 39% and 80% of slide height running behind the cards. Title is top-left at y 7.7%, subtitle beneath it at y 15.4%, the Innovate badge sits top-right, the sonova logo is bottom-left and the page number is bottom-right, with margins of about 3% on left and right.

**Visuals**

Four line icons in white, each inside a coloured card header, plus a lightbulb icon in a white circle at the top right. The compass-style drafting icon (dividers with a pen tip) sits at the bottom-right of the Design-first header. The heart with an ECG pulse line sits at the bottom-right of the Healthy-aging header. The gear over a laptop with a document and a smile-less tablet base sits at the bottom-right of the Software business model header. A smartphone outline with a smiling face sits at the bottom-right of the Cohesive digital journey header. The lightbulb with rays is a dark navy outline inside a white circular badge at top right. No photographs or charts. Two thin blue rules, about 0.2% of slide height thick, run horizontally: one from the right edge of the Design-first card to the right edge of the slide at about 39% height, and one at about 80% height from the left margin to the Cohesive body card.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background with navy and cerulean blue accents. Titles are cerulean #007FBE in Roboto Regular, about 4.5% of slide height. Subtitle and body text are navy #003C64 in Roboto Regular; body text about 2.2% of slide height. Card headers are filled navy #003C64 (left column) and cerulean #007FBE (right column) with white Roboto Regular text about 3% of slide height. Body cards are white with a thin light grey soft drop shadow and small corner radius (about 1% of slide width). Header rectangles have very small square-to-slight corner radius and no border. The Innovate badge has a navy #003C64 outline about 0.3% of slide height thick with a small corner radius and white fill; the bold Roboto label is navy #003C64. The page number is grey #757266.

Multi-stage approach to win in Asia / Innovate for adoption / Clear share upside / Upside potential / Europe / North America / Asia / Sonova market share / Attractive fundamentals / Favorable demographics / Low adoption rate / Rising income level / Phased growth strategy for Asia / 1 / Leverage and scale existing portfolio for affluent segments in Asia / 2 / Resource deployment / Develop and market “made for Asia” solutions (product and cost-efficient care models) / sonova / HEAR THE WORLD / 19

**Layout**

Single 16:9 slide with a title top-left at about 8.6% from the top and a sonova logo bottom-left, with a page number bottom-right. Upper half holds two columns: a bar chart with its heading at left (about x 2.8 to 32%) and three stacked cards with heading at right (about x 54 to 94%). A full-width dark blue band spans y 54.8 to 89.2% and holds a phased strategy with a heading and two chevron rows; a blue circle with a down chevron straddles the band's top edge at centre-left. Left margin is about 3%, right margin about 4 to 5%.

**Visuals**

Bar chart titled 'Clear share upside' with three vertical bars in #007FBE, no value labels and no axis numbers. Categories left to right: Europe (about top at 34.4% of slide height, the tallest), North America (about top at 36.4%, slightly shorter), Asia (about top at 42.0%, the shortest). Bars share a baseline at y 50.2% drawn as a thin #003C64 horizontal axis line from about x 3 to 32%. A dark navy #003C64 up arrow above the Asia bar, with the label 'Upside potential' above it, marks the gap to the top. Legend at right of the chart: a small #007FBE square followed by 'Sonova market share' in #003C64. Three white rounded cards with soft grey drop shadows, each about 13% wide and 16% tall, containing light blue text. Above-right, a white circle with a thin #003C64 lightbulb icon and a navy #003C64 outlined rounded rectangle labelled 'Innovate for adoption'. Centre-left: a #007FBE circle (about 4.7% wide) with a white down chevron. Blue band background is a dark-to-mid blue (#003C64 to #0A6FAF) abstract wave-line photograph/illustration with faint lighter curves. Strategy chevrons: row 1 is a #003C64 right-pointing chevron-tipped bar with a white 1px outline containing the bold white text, with a numbered box '1' at left (#003C64 fill, white 1px outline). Row 2 has a numbered box '2' at left, then a white box with a dashed grey outline containing 'Resource deployment' in navy, then a #003C64 chevron bar with white outline containing the bold white text. A white line runs under the 'Phased growth strategy for Asia' heading. Sonova logo bottom-left: blue (#007FBE) bold italic lowercase 'sonova' wordmark with 'HEAR THE WORLD' tagline beneath in small caps.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF with a full-width dark-to-mid blue (#003C64 to #0A6FAF) gradient band with abstract wave pattern across y 54.8 to 89.2%. White background (#FFFFFF) with a blue gradient band in the lower-middle. Titles are light-weight blue #007FBE, body labels are navy #003C64, section headings are #0083CA with a thin blue underline. Typeface is Roboto (sans-serif): Regular for titles, headings and body; Bold for the strategy statements and the 'Innovate for adoption' label. Title text is about 4.8% of slide height, section headings about 3.3%, body and labels about 1.8 to 2.6%. Cards have white fill, very slight rounded corners (about 0.3% radius) and soft shadows. Chevron rows have sharp corners with a 1px white outline. Blue accents #007FBE; navy #003C64 for structure. Page number is grey #757266.

**Layout**

Two-column grid on a 2000 by 1125 px slide: a left column of two stacked white panels (top panel about 25 to 58% of height, bottom panel about 60 to 90%) and a right tall white panel (about 25 to 90% of height, x 52 to 97%). The title sits top-left at x 2.9%, y 6%, left-aligned, two lines. A boxed tag with a circular icon sits top-right. A blue circular chevron button straddles the gap between the left and right panels at about y 56 to 62%. The sonova logo sits bottom-left at x 3%, y 95%; the page number sits bottom-right at x 95 to 96%, y 96%. Left margin about 3%, right margin about 3%.

**Visuals**

Four donut charts in the top-left panel, labelled US, FR, UK and CN, each with a white centre hole carrying the country code. All values are unprinted, so estimates are marked about. US: about 45% Independents (dark navy, from 12 o'clock clockwise to about 6 o'clock), about 40% Large retailers and Multinationals (mid blue), about 15% Government/VA (light blue, 10 to 12 o'clock). FR: about 55% Independents (navy, right and bottom), about 45% Large retailers and Multinationals (mid blue, left). UK: about 85% Government/VA (light blue, large arc), about 10% Large retailers and Multinationals (mid blue, upper right), about 3% Independents (navy, small sliver at 12 o'clock). CN: about 75% Large retailers and Multinationals (mid blue, large arc), about 12% Independents (navy, upper right), about 12% Government/VA (light blue, upper left). Donut segments are separated by thin white gaps. Legend below the donuts: navy square Independents, mid blue square Large retailers & Multinationals, light blue square Government/VA. Bottom-left panel: a grouped vertical bar chart with no axis labels, no tick values and no printed numbers; baseline is a thin navy horizontal line. Groups by category, each with three bars in order navy, mid blue, light blue; heights are about relative. Independent: navy about 70% of max, mid blue about 35%, light blue about 15%. Large retailers and Multinationals: navy about 50%, mid blue about 85%, light blue about 35%. VA: navy about 85%, mid blue about 85%, light blue about 8%. Government/Reimbursement: navy about 35%, mid blue about 35%, light blue about 85%. Legend below: navy square Audiological performance, mid blue square Workflow efficiency, light blue square Value solution. Right panel: a stepped four-tier pyramid diagram, apex at top centre, base at bottom, tiers separated by thin mid blue step outlines. Tier fill is a vertical gradient from light blue #6EB4DC at the apex to white at the base. Tier labels from top: Brand A, Brand B, Brand C, Brand D. The step lines run horizontally from the left edge, step down at about 76%, 75% and 73% of the width and continue to the right edge. Top-right icon: a line-drawn stacked triangle icon in navy inside a white circle with a thin grey shadow, next to a navy-outlined rounded rectangle tag. Blue circular button with white right-pointing chevron centred on the gap between panels. Sonova logo: bold italic lowercase wordmark in blue with the small tagline HEAR THE WORLD beneath it.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean corporate look on white. Title in Roboto Regular, sentence case, blue #007FBE, about 4.8% of slide height, two lines with tight leading. Panel headers are white Roboto Regular text on navy bars, about 2.6% of height. Body labels are Roboto Regular navy #003C64 about 1.8% of height. 'Illustrative' labels are Roboto Italic navy #003C64 about 2.2% of height. Page number Roboto Regular grey #757266 about 1.7% of height. Palette: navy #003C64, mid blue #007FBE, light blue #6EB4DC, white #FFFFFF. Panels are white rounded rectangles with small corner radius and soft grey drop shadow. Header bars are navy rectangles with small corner radius. Pyramid outline in mid blue #007FBE, thin stroke about 1 px. Tag box has a navy #003C64 border about 2 px, small corner radius, white fill.

### Slide 21

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Uses a before-and-after layout to show how a single product launch flows through retail and wholesale channels.
- Review: The deck follows a clear route (agenda, introduction, strategy update, conclusion) and keeps one consistent identity: blue palette, section-tag badges on slides 14 to 25, and speaker divider slides.
- Review: Slides 8, 21 and 25 are well built: labelled charts, a before and after proof of concept, and a lever strip tied to a CHF 50m and CHF 90m figure.
- Worth keeping: Keep the consistent section-badge system, the four-step lever strip on slide 25, and the R&D to Retail to Patient flow with before and after proof on slide 21.

**Text**

Unlock “Wholesale – Retail” synergies / Multi-channel, / multi-brand / For / Innovation / Direct patient access / through own retail / R&D / Product / Retail / Fitting / Patient / Data & feedback / Faster product feedback loop / For / Market activation / Example: / Virto-R launch / Global marketing / assets / deployed in own / stores, showcasing / … and at wholesale / partners / Coordinated launch / campaigns across / channels / Optimized brand and product / presence / For / Consumer reach / Leveraging our Lead / Generation Factory / (LGF) / Before / LGF creates leads for / own retail … / 11+ / countries served / and expanding / +20% / Return on / Ad Spend / After / ...for wholesale partners where Sonova has no store / Proof of Concept: / >100 / appointments / in first 4-week pilot / Next pilot markets / and more / Market share gain / sonova / HEAR THE WORLD / 21

**Layout**

Three horizontal bands separated by thin light-blue rules at about 44.5% and 68% of slide height. Each band has a bold row label block in the left column (about x 3% to 20%), a bordered diagram or panel in the middle column (about x 17% to 90%), and a bordered white card with a blue chevron circle on its left edge in the right column (x 72.6% to 97%). The title sits top left at y 7.5% with a badge top right; the sonova logo sits bottom left and the page number 21 sits bottom right, both on a margin about 3% from the sides.

**Visuals**

Diagram in the Innovation band: three rounded-rectangle nodes in a row, R&D (dark navy #003C64 fill, white bold label) with a straight arrow labelled Product to Retail (mid blue #007FBE fill, white bold label), then a straight arrow labelled Fitting to Patient (mid blue #007FBE fill, white bold label). A dashed blue #007FBE line starts under Patient, runs left along a horizontal path and rises into an arrowhead under R&D, labelled Data & feedback beneath it. Market activation band: a navy #003C64 callout box labelled Example: / Virto-R launch, then three photographs in a row: (1) Global marketing assets, a glossy black hearing-aid device with a reflective white surface below, close-up, cropped at the edges with a neutral white background; (2) deployed in own stores, showcasing, a daylight street-level photo of an AudioNova store front with green signage and a green-framed glass entrance, slightly cropped, boxed with small rounded corners; (3) ... and at wholesale partners, a printed German leaflet with a white page, black headline text, a green circular product inset and a Hörcentrum Müller logo, boxed. Consumer reach band: Before panel with a navy Before tag, the text LGF creates leads for own retail … and two stats 11+ and +20%; After panel with a navy After tag, a navy Proof of Concept label with an Italian flag (green, white, red vertical tricolour), the stat >100 with its caption, a small blue chevron pointing right, and a line-art globe icon in mid blue #007FBE with a paper-plane-like swoosh labelled Next pilot markets and more. Badge top right: a white circle with a thin navy outline containing a navy triangle outline icon, next to a navy-outlined rectangle reading Multi-channel, multi-brand. Logo: the sonova wordmark in mid blue italic with the tagline HEAR THE WORLD beneath.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white #FFFFFF background with no gradients. Title in Roboto Regular, mid blue #007FBE, large, roughly 4.8% of slide height. Row labels and stat numbers in Roboto Bold, navy #003C64, about 3% of slide height. Body captions in Roboto Regular, navy #003C64, about 1.8 to 2.6% of slide height. Accents are mid blue #007FBE (chevron circles, arrows, outlines, flow boxes) and navy #003C64 (tags, Example box, label blocks). Outlines are thin 1px strokes in #007FBE with small corner radii, about 0.5% of slide width. Right-column cards are white with light grey-blue #B8C4D0 borders and a soft drop shadow. Horizontal rules are 1px light blue #A9C6DE. Page number in Roboto Regular grey #757266.

### Slide 22

**Review notes**

- Review: The deck follows a clear route (agenda, introduction, strategy update, conclusion) and keeps one consistent identity: blue palette, section-tag badges on slides 14 to 25, and speaker divider slides.

**Text**

Scaling retail network in strategic markets
Geo-targeted expansion and optimization
Multi-channel,
multi-brand
Scale matters
Leverage fixed cost base
Stronger brand visibility
Cost per lead
Talent attraction and
retention
Example: Germany
>80%
Brand awareness
Aided brand awareness
among 70+ years old (doubled
in the past 10 years)
190K
Lead Generation Factory
Qualified consumer leads
generated in DE in
FY 25/26 (>600k globally)
++
Profitability
Accretive
Expanding retail beyond
bolt-ons through larger
acquisitions in strategic
markets
sonova
HEAR THE WORLD
22

**Layout**

Title sits top-left at about a 3% left margin, with a grey-blue subtitle directly beneath it. A row of three white rounded cards spans the lower body from about 26% to 89% of the slide height: a narrow left card, a wider centre card and a medium right card, separated by small gaps, with a blue arrow circle straddling the gap between the centre and right cards. The multi-channel tag sits top-right, the sonova logo bottom-left and the page number bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Four navy outline check-mark icons sit in circles at the left of the 'Scale matters' card, one beside each of its four items. The centre card contains three stat rows, each with a navy label pill: '>80%' (Brand awareness), '190K' (Lead Generation Factory) and '++' (Profitability). There is no chart, table or photograph. A solid blue circle (#0E82C6) with a white right-pointing chevron sits in the gap between the middle and right cards. Top-right, a white circle with a thin navy outline contains a navy outlined triangle with a horizontal line across its lower third, and it is joined to a navy outlined rounded rectangle holding the tag text. The sonova logo is a blue wordmark with the tagline HEAR THE WORLD beneath it.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF covering the full slide. Clean corporate style on a plain white background. Titles are blue #007FBE in Roboto Regular, and the subtitle, body text and stat labels are navy #003C64. Section headings use a lighter blue #0E82C6. Roboto Bold is used for emphasis in the right card and in the white label text on navy pills. The title is about 4.8% of slide height and body text about 3.0%. Navy pills (#003C64) have fully rounded ends. Cards are white with a soft grey drop shadow and small corner radii, and there are no gradients. The check icons use navy strokes of about 2px width.

### Slide 25

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Breaks productivity into four named levers, each with short bullet detail and a clear savings target.
- Review: The deck follows a clear route (agenda, introduction, strategy update, conclusion) and keeps one consistent identity: blue palette, section-tag badges on slides 14 to 25, and speaker divider slides.
- Review: Slides 8, 21 and 25 are well built: labelled charts, a before and after proof of concept, and a lever strip tied to a CHF 50m and CHF 90m figure.
- Worth keeping: Keep the consistent section-badge system, the four-step lever strip on slide 25, and the R&D to Retail to Patient flow with before and after proof on slide 21.

**Text**

Four strategic levers to improve productivity
Excel in operations
Footprint
Processes
Automation
Value engineering
Clear sites by mandate
Simplified logistics
Decoupled physical vs. information vs. financial flows
End-to-end flows
Effective forecast-to-demand-to-planning process
After sales and order entry
Handling and sorting
Make-or-buy decision
(Re)design to cost
Multiple sourcing
Transformation investment
of CHF 50m
total over the next 3 years
Savings vs FY 25/26 baseline
of around CHF 90m p.a.
exit run rate year 4
sonova
HEAR THE WORLD
25

**Layout**

Title sits top-left at about 3% margin, with a boxed 'Excel in operations' badge at top-right. A full-width row of four equal-height coloured tabs sits at mid-upper height, each with an icon and label, followed by four left-aligned bullet columns separated by thin vertical rules. A wide white rounded panel with a looping arrow frame spans the lower part, with two text blocks (right-aligned left half, left-aligned right half). Sonova logo bottom-left and page number bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Four horizontal tabs forming a process strip, left to right: Footprint (blue #007FBE tab with white line icon of a footprint with toe dots), Processes (dark navy #003C64 tab with white line icon of three interlocking gears), Automation (light blue #6EB4DC tab with white line icon of a hand/robot arm under a wifi-style signal), Value engineering (blue #007FBE tab with white line icon of a ring/circle with a small arc). Each tab has a semicircular notch bulging into the next tab. Top-right badge: white circle with soft shadow containing a navy line icon of a gear with a rising trend line, overlapping a navy-outlined rounded rectangle carrying the text 'Excel in operations'. Bottom panel: white rounded rectangle with soft grey shadow containing a navy outline arrow path: a horizontal line from the upper-left running right with an arrowhead at the upper-right, down the right edge, a horizontal return line along the bottom with an arrowhead pointing left, and a vertical line up the left edge, forming a loop around the two text blocks. Sonova wordmark logo in blue #007FBE bold italic with tagline 'HEAR THE WORLD' beneath. No photographs, charts or tables.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean corporate style on white. Title in Roboto Regular, blue #007FBE, approximately 4.8% of slide height, sentence case. Tab labels in Roboto Regular white, about 3.0% of slide height. Column bullets in Roboto Regular navy #003C64, about 2.6% of slide height, with small blue #007FBE round bullets. Bottom headline figures in Roboto Bold: 'Transformation investment' navy #003C64 about 3.7%, 'Savings vs FY 25/26 baseline' blue #007FBE about 3.7%; supporting lines in Roboto Regular about 3.7% (navy and blue) and 2.6% for small captions. Badge text Roboto Bold navy #003C64. Tabs are flat solid fills with rounded outer corners (radius about 1.5% of slide height) and semicircle notches; no gradients. Bottom panel has a soft diffuse drop shadow. Thin vertical divider lines in blue #007FBE, about 0.15% width. Arrow loop strokes navy #003C64, about 0.15% of slide height, with small arrowheads.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

- shape at x 0, y 0, w 100, h 100: Full-slide background rectangle (fill #FFFFFF, no outline)

### Slide 26

**Text**

Elodie Carr-Cingari
CFO
26

**Layout**

Single centred title block on a white slide: the name sits at the vertical middle, with the role line 'CFO' right-aligned beneath it at its right edge. The sonova logo sits bottom-left and the slide number bottom-right, both at small margins from the slide edges. No header or grid is visible.

**Visuals**

Decorative background illustration: a field of thin, flowing, wave-like curved lines in pale light blue, sweeping across the full slide from upper-left to lower-right and rising on the right edge. Lines are very faint, with denser clusters near the centre-left and right, and fade to white toward the edges. Full-bleed, no cropping visible, no tint overlay. Logo: sonova wordmark in lowercase blue italic letters with the tagline 'HEAR THE WORLD' in small capitals beneath, bottom-left, colour #007FBE. No other icons or photographs.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF with a full-bleed faint light-blue wave-line illustration. White background #FFFFFF with a full-bleed faint light-blue wave-line pattern. Name and role text in blue #007FBE. Typeface Roboto Regular, sans-serif, normal weight, sentence case, no letter-spacing. Name is about 8.2% of slide height, role about 4.4% of slide height, slide number about 1.7% of slide height in grey #757266. Shapes: none besides the wave lines, no corner radii, shadows or gradients on text elements.

### Slide 27

**Text**

Trading update – Continued share gains in Wholesale supporting growth in 2H / Wholesale / (Hearing Instruments) / • Strong growth – well above market / • Momentum driven by successful product launches / Retail / (Audiological Care) / • Continued organic growth (vs. higher comparison) / • Limited contribution from M&A / FY 2025/26 guidance / maintained / Growth expected to be / at the lower end of the / respective guidance ranges / Consumer Hearing / • Return to growth in 2H / • Business to be treated as discontinued operations as of the FY 25/26 results / Cochlear Implants / • Continued headwind from VBP introduction in China / • Upgrade sales decline reflecting product cycle combined with increased competitive pressure / sonova / HEAR THE WORLD / 27

**Layout**

Slide is a 2x2 grid of white cards on the left (Wholesale top-left, Retail top-right, Consumer Hearing bottom-left, Cochlear Implants bottom-right) with a blue circular arrow between the grid and a single larger white card on the right holding the guidance statement. The title sits top-left across the full width at about 7.5% from the top, left-aligned with a margin of about 2.9% from the left edge. The Sonova logo sits bottom-left and the slide number bottom-right, both in the bottom margin.

**Visuals**

Four white rounded-corner cards with thin light-grey borders and soft drop shadows form a 2x2 grid of equal size (about 27.6% by 26.7% of the slide each). To the right, one taller white card with the same border and shadow treatment holds the guidance statement. Between the grid and this card sits a solid blue (#007FBE) circle about 3.5% wide with a white right-pointing chevron, indicating progression from the grid to the guidance. No photographs, charts or tables. The Sonova wordmark in blue is the only logo.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradient or pattern. Title in Roboto Regular, sentence case, colour #007FBE, about 4.8% of slide height. Card headings in Roboto Bold, colour #003C64, about 2.6% of slide height. Card body text in Roboto Regular, colour #003C64, about 2.6% of slide height, with blue #007FBE bullet dots. Right card headline in Roboto Bold #003C64 about 4.4% of slide height; its sub-statement in Roboto Regular #003C64 about 3.7% of slide height. Cards have white fill, thin light grey-blue outlines, small corner radius and soft grey drop shadows. Arrow circle is solid #007FBE with a white chevron. Page number in Roboto Regular, grey #757266.

**Layout**

Single-slide content layout on a white field with a title at top left (x 2.9%, y 7.5%) and a two-part KPI band beneath it: two grey panels side by side, each with a blue or navy vertical accent bar at its left edge. Below them, three equal-height rounded cards sit in a row of three columns (roughly 3.4%, 35.9% and 66.9% left edges, each about 28% wide, spanning y 41.6% to 83.2%), with card titles at the top left of each card and bullet lists beneath. A footnote runs across the bottom-left at y 87.4%, the sonova logo sits bottom-left at y about 95%, and the page number 28 sits bottom-right at x 95.2%, y 95.9%. Left margin is about 3.4% of slide width.

**Visuals**

none

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean corporate style on white. Title and subtitle in medium blue #007FBE in Roboto Regular, about 4.8% of slide height for the title and 2.6% for the parenthetical subtitle. KPI labels are Roboto Bold in blue #0283CA (SALES CAGR) and navy #003C64 (EBIT CAGR), about 3.3% of height. KPI values are large Roboto Regular, about 6.7% of height, in blue #0283CA for sales and navy #003C64 for EBIT. Card titles are Roboto Regular about 3.7% of height in #0E82C6. Body bullets are Roboto Regular about 2.6% of height in navy #003C64, with bold key phrases; bullet glyphs are Arial-style round dots in #0283CA. Panels are flat #F2F2F2 with no border. Accent bars are solid with no shadow. Cards are white or #DEDDD9 with thin light grey #BFBFBF rounded outlines, corner radius about 1.5% of slide width, and a soft drop shadow. The first card is filled #DEDDD9 and the other two are white. Footnote is Roboto Regular about 2.0% of height in navy #003C64. Page number is Roboto Regular about 1.7% of height in grey #757266.

### Slide 29

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Builds the CHF 6bn ambition as a stacked set of growth engines, making the arithmetic of the target easy to follow.

**Text**

Multiple growth engines driving the path toward CHF 6bn sales ambition
Larger acquisitions / & other strategic / opportunities
Sales
Sales
Share gains / through innovation / & geographic / expansion
Bolt-on M&A / and greenfield
1-2%
1-3%
Market growth
3-5%
Sales
CAGR (LC) / 5% to 10%
CHF 6bn / ambition
Sales
FY 25/26
Mid-term target
FY 30/31
29

**Layout**

Single title line across the top at the left margin (x 2.9%, y 7.5%), with a large empty band beneath it. A rounded white card spans the slide width (x 2.9% to 97.1%, y 26.9% to 78.9%) holding a waterfall of seven columns that step upward from left to right, with each bar on a shared baseline near 78%. Sonova logo bottom-left at about x 3%, y 95%; page number bottom-right at x 95.2%, y 95.9%.

**Visuals**

Waterfall (bridge) chart with no axis, gridlines or numeric scale. Columns left to right: (1) total bar 'Sales' FY 25/26, navy #003C64, x 9.0 to 18.5%, top at y 64.2%; (2) step 'Market growth' with value 3-5%, blue #007FBE, x 20.0 to 31.0%, y 55.4 to 64.4%; (3) step 'Share gains through innovation & geographic expansion' with value 1-3%, blue #007FBE, x 32.4 to 43.4%, y 49.7 to 55.7%; (4) step 'Bolt-on M&A and greenfield' with value 1-2%, blue #007FBE, x 44.8 to 55.8%, y 45.8 to 49.9%; (5) total bar 'Sales' with label 'CAGR (LC) 5% to 10%', navy, x 57.5 to 67.0%, y 45.8 to 78.6%; (6) step 'Larger acquisitions & other strategic opportunities' with no value printed, blue, x 68.5 to 79.5%, y 41.9 to 46.0%, about 2 to 5 percent of sales by bar height; (7) total bar 'Sales' with label 'CHF 6bn ambition', navy, x 81.1 to 90.6%, top at y 41.9%. Each step bar starts at the top of the previous bar, joined by fine dotted connector lines in blue. Totals 1, 5 and 7 carry white double diagonal axis-break slashes near y 70 to 74%, and the bars continue below them to the pills. Three light-blue pill labels sit under the bars, each overlapping the base of its column: 'FY 25/26' (under bar 1), 'Mid-term target' (under bar 5, wider), 'FY 30/31' (under bar 7). Thin light-blue baseline strokes run under each pill. Lighting and photography: none; all flat fills.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white slide with a light grey rounded card and soft shadow behind the chart. Title in Roboto Regular, blue #007FBE, sans-serif, about 4.8% of slide height, with no bold. Chart labels in Roboto Regular, navy #003C64, about 2.2% of slide height; totals and pill labels in Roboto Bold, navy #003C64, about 2.6%; values inside blue bars and total bars in white Roboto, 2.6%. Accents: blue #007FBE for steps, light blue #6EB4DC for pills and baselines. Page number in grey #757266, about 1.7%. Card has corner radius of roughly 2% of slide width, light grey hairline border and a soft drop shadow. No gradients.

**Layout**

A single-line title runs across the top of the slide from the left margin, at about 7.5% of the slide height. A large rounded white panel with a thin grey border and soft shadow fills the middle band from about 26% to 79% of the height. Inside it, six vertical waterfall bars step up left to right, with the first and last bars anchored near the panel bottom. The FY 25/26 and Mid-term target tags sit on the panel's bottom edge. The sonova logo is bottom-left and the page number bottom-right, both at about 96% of the height.

**Visuals**

Waterfall chart, untitled apart from the slide title, drawn as a bridge from Core EBIT FY 25/26 to Core EBIT Mid-term target. Categories left to right: Core EBIT FY 25/26 (dark navy bar, no value printed); Sales growth 5-10% (mid blue bar, floating above the first bar); Operating leverage from growth and innovation ~1-2% (mid blue bar); Operations footprint and supply chain ~2-3% (mid blue bar); Strategic investments (R&D, Operations, IT and digitalization) ~ -1-3% (light grey bar, shown as a negative step); Core EBIT Mid-term target, CAGR (LC) 7% to 12% (dark navy bar, tallest). The first and last bars carry white diagonal break marks near their lower third, indicating truncated axes. No axes, scale values or legend are printed. Connectors are thin dotted blue lines running horizontally between the top or bottom edge of each step. Bar heights are not printed beyond the labelled ranges. The Core EBIT bars are the only navy fills, the growth steps are #007FBE and the negative step is #DEDDD9. Sonova logo is blue wordmark, bottom-left.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean corporate style on a white background. Title in Roboto Regular, mid blue #007FBE, about 4.8% of slide height, sentence case. Labels and body text in Roboto Regular, dark navy #003C64, about 2.2% of slide height; value labels in white Roboto Regular about 2.6%; totals and tags in Roboto Bold about 2.6%. Accents: mid blue #007FBE for growth bars, light blue #6EB4DC for tags and cap lines, light grey #DEDDD9 for the negative step, grey #757266 for the page number. Panel has rounded corners of roughly 3% of slide width, a light grey outline and a soft drop shadow. Bars are flat with no gradients or shadows. Dotted connectors are thin blue lines.

**Layout**

Single white slide with a title across the top-left at about 7.5% from the top, spanning nearly the full width with a left margin of about 3%. The content sits in two columns of cards on the left (two donut cards side by side above a full-width outlined box) and one tall card on the right, separated by a narrow gutter holding two circular chevron arrows and a dashed divider line. The sonova logo sits bottom-left and the page number bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Two donut charts (Sales currency mix and Cost currency mix) and two circular chevron arrow icons. Sales currency mix donut: no printed values; segments clockwise from 12 o'clock are CHF (dark navy #003C64, about 1%, a sliver just right of 12 o'clock), EUR (#007FBE, about 33%), USD (#6EB4DC, about 25%) and Other (#D6D7D9 light grey, about 25%), with the labels CHF, EUR, USD and Other printed outside the ring. Caption beneath: CHF ~1% of Global Sales. Cost currency mix donut: no printed values; segments clockwise from 12 o'clock are CHF (#003C64, about 10%), EUR (#007FBE, about 30%), USD (#6EB4DC, about 25%) and Other (#D6D7D9, about 25%), labels CHF, EUR, USD and Other printed outside the ring. Caption beneath: CHF ~15% of Global Cost Base. Neither donut has a legend or highlighted points; the rings have a white centre hole. Chevron icons: dark navy circle #003C64 with white right-pointing chevron at upper right of gutter, and a mid-blue circle #007FBE with white right-pointing chevron below it. A dashed blue line #007FBE runs horizontally across the gutter and into the right card at about 63.5% height. Checkmarks are small navy ticks in front of each bullet on the right card. Sonova logo: wordmark sonova in bold lowercase italic blue #007FBE with tagline HEAR THE WORLD beneath. Two images are placed in the file behind the right card (759 by 761 and 253 by 254 pixels, roughly the right card's area); they are not visibly distinct on the slide and read as soft card shadow.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Flat white background with no gradient or texture. Titles and accent text in blue #007FBE; body and bullet text in navy #003C64; the donut cards use navy label chips with white text. Typeface is Roboto (sans-serif), regular for title and body, bold for card headings and the CHF figures. Title is about 4.8% of slide height; body text about 2.6% and small labels about 2.2%. Cards are white rounded rectangles with a thin soft grey drop shadow and small corner radius. The bottom-left box has a 1 pt blue #007FBE outline with rounded corners and no fill. Chevron circles are solid filled. Dashed divider in blue #007FBE, thin. Page number in grey #757266.

### Slide 32

**Text**

Disciplined capital allocation supporting growth and shareholder returns
1 Organic growth investments
• Step-up R&D to accelerate roadmap
• Invest >1% of sales in digital & IT initiative
• CHF 50m investment program to drive operational efficiency
• Capex @ 3.5-4.0% of revenue
2 Retail bolt-ons
Scaling store footprint: CHF 80-100m p.a.
3 Strategic M&A
• Selective larger acquisitions in strategic countries
• Technology acquisitions
4 Attractive dividend
Maintain payout ratio of around 40%
5 Healthy balance sheet
Targeting net debt/EBITDA ratio of 1.0-1.5x
6 Share buyback
• No buyback foreseen in FY 26/27 due to strong M&A pipeline
• Review thereafter once leverage target consistently met
sonova HEAR THE WORLD
32

**Layout**

Title sits top-left at the top margin, spanning most of the width, with no subtitle. Below it, six white rounded cards sit in two rows on a 3-column grid: row one has three cards (the first two rows of columns are wider, 33.6% and 28.8% of width) and row two has three cards, with the first and last wider; each card has a numbered header band above its body. The sonova logo sits bottom-left and the page number sits bottom-right, both at the footer line.

**Visuals**

Six white rounded rectangle cards with thin outlines and soft drop shadows, each with a filled circular number badge (1 to 6) and a header band. No photographs, charts, icons or diagrams. The sonova wordmark in blue with the tagline HEAR THE WORLD beneath it forms the logo at bottom-left.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background; title and card headers in blue #007FBE and navy #003C64; body text navy #003C64; accents blue #0283CA and dark navy #003C64 for number badges (1 to 3 navy, 4 to 6 blue). Typeface Roboto (sans-serif) with Regular and Bold weights; title about 4.8% of slide height, card headings about 3.3%, body about 2.6%. Cards have 0.2% thin outlines in navy for row one and blue for row two, with rounded corners of about 1.5% and soft shadows. Bullets are small blue dots.

**Layout**

Three equal white cards sit in a horizontal row across the lower two-thirds of the slide, with a five-row label column on the far left that aligns with the rows of the first card. The title sits top-left at the top margin, left-aligned, with generous empty space between title and cards. Two blue circular chevron arrows sit in the gutters between the cards, vertically centred on the middle of the cards. The sonova logo sits bottom-left and the page number bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Three identical rounded white cards (608 by 753 px source images, shown as cards) with a faint grey outline and soft drop shadow. Card 1 (left, 22.5% to 47.2% horizontally) holds the 2021–2025 financial track record as five stacked values aligned to the row labels: High single-digit CAGR (LC); >20% average Core EBIT margin; ~90%; 1.0-1.5x Net debt / EBITDA; 18–20% ROCE. Card 2 (centre, 47.7% to 72.4%) is text-only with no chart. Card 3 (right, 73.0% to 97.7%) lists Sales CAGR (LC) 5% to 10% and Core EBIT CAGR (LC) 7% to 12%, each value between two thin dark-blue horizontal rules. Two solid blue (#0A7FC1) circles with white right-pointing chevron outlines sit between the cards, one between card 1 and card 2 and one between card 2 and card 3, each linking the cards left to right. Five light grey dotted horizontal rules run across the first card at the row boundaries, from the row label column into the card. No photographs, charts or tables.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF.

**Layout**

A single section-divider line of text sits on a full-bleed background with no title placeholder, footer, or slide number. The title box spans x 31.8 to 61.3 percent and y 39.5 to 47.8 percent, so it sits just left of centre and slightly above the vertical middle, with its text left-aligned within the box.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed illustration of flowing wave lines: dozens of thin, evenly spaced, semi-transparent light-blue curves sweep from the upper left and upper right down toward the lower right and across the middle. The lines are densest in the upper-left and lower-right areas and fade into the darker blue around the centre-left. Lighting is uniform with no highlights. The lines are lighter blue (about #1E8AD6) than the surrounding gradient. The image covers the entire slide and is not cropped, boxed, or tinted beyond its own colours.

**Design**

Background is a diagonal gradient from deep blue #003C64 at the lower left to brighter blue about #0F7FC8 at the upper right, overlaid with the wave-line illustration. Title text is white #FFFFFF in Roboto Regular (sans-serif, regular weight, no italics, no capitals, normal letter-spacing), at about 8.16 percent of slide height. There is no body text, accent colour, border, shadow, or corner radius.

### Slide 35

**Text**

To conclude
A clear ambition of reaching CHF 6 billion
in revenues by FY 2030/31
Focus on hearing care
Confidence from clear strategic direction, tech
leadership, talents and financial strength
sonova
HEAR THE WORLD
35

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white field. The title 'To conclude' sits top-left at about 7.5% from the top, left-aligned at x 2.9%. Three stacked white message cards run down the left half (x 3.1% to 48.9%), left-aligned text inside each card, with even vertical gaps of about 8%. A tall hearing-aid photograph fills the right column from top to bottom edge (x 58.1% to 99.1%). The sonova logo sits bottom-left and the page number sits bottom-right, both on a common baseline near 96%.

**Visuals**

Three white rectangular message cards on the left, each with a thin pale blue-grey outline and a soft grey drop shadow, no rounded corners. One photograph on the right: a silver-chrome in-the-ear hearing aid lying vertically, shown in side profile with a transparent cut-away window revealing the black and gold circuit board, microphone port and battery contacts. A clear rubber ear tip with a grey-black dome is attached at the lower right by a short translucent tube. The lower-left of the body shows a glossy cylindrical component. The photo is on a white studio background, lit from the upper left with soft specular highlights on the chrome, dominant colours silver grey, black and white with small blue-grey tints; it is full-height and not boxed. Nothing overlays the photo.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradient or texture. Title and card text are in Roboto in brilliant corporate blue #007FBE; card text is Roboto Regular with key phrases in Roboto Bold. Title is Roboto Regular at about 4.8% of slide height; body text is about 3.3% of slide height. Cards have white fill, 1 pt pale grey-blue outline (about #C9D9E6), square corners and a soft shadow offset downwards. Page number is grey. Logo is blue. No gradients.

### Slide 36

**Text**

sonova
HEAR THE WORLD

**Layout**

Single full-bleed title-style slide with no title placeholder, footer or slide number. The sonova logo lockup sits left of centre, its left edge at about 9.5% of slide width and its vertical centre at about 58% of slide height. The wordmark is left-aligned above the tagline, which is right-aligned to the wordmark's right edge. The right half of the slide is empty apart from the background wave lines.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed abstract illustration of many fine, thin, light-blue (about #2A8FD8) curved lines forming two flowing wave bands. One band sweeps from the upper left down to the lower left, and a second sweeps from the top centre-right down to the bottom right, with the lines dense and bright near the top and fading toward the lower right. The lines are semi-transparent and overlay the blue gradient background. The wave lines are not cropped and are not boxed. Logo: white sonova wordmark in lowercase rounded italic letterforms, with the tagline HEAR THE WORLD in white uppercase sans-serif beneath it.

**Design**

Background is a diagonal blue gradient from deep blue at the bottom left (about #04507F) to brighter cerulean at the top right (about #1190D8), with a mid-tone around #0A6AAE at the centre. Wordmark and tagline are white (#FFFFFF). The wave lines are thin light-blue strokes of about 1 px at this resolution, colour about #2A8FD8 at roughly 40% opacity. No shadows, corner radii or boxes. Wordmark is a custom rounded italic sans-serif with heavy weight, roughly 16% of slide height in x-height terms. Tagline is a regular-weight condensed-looking humanist sans-serif in capitals, cap height about 4% of slide height, about 5.7% em, with slight letter spacing.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

- illustration at x 0, y 0, w 100, h 100 [#2A8FD8]: Full-bleed abstract wave-line pattern of thin light-blue curved strokes over the gradient background (Lines are semi-transparent (about 40% opacity), about 1 px wide, densest at the top and sweeping left and right; no boxes, shadows or cropping beyond slide edges)
- logo at x 9.5, y 50, w 36.9, h 12 [custom rounded italic sans-serif, bold; font size 16% of slide height; #FFFFFF; left aligned]: sonova (Lowercase wordmark, white, sits above the tagline and is left-aligned to the lockup)
- subtitle at x 21.8, y 65, w 23.9, h 4 [humanist sans-serif, regular weight, all capitals, slight letter-spacing; font size 5.7% of slide height; #FFFFFF; right aligned]: HEAR THE WORLD (Single line, right-aligned to the wordmark's right edge)
